# iPhone Duo implementation checklist

## Current completion checklist: 25 October 2026

**No full-suite pass.** Slider source changes are committed in `46b48a8e`; the repeatable test and guide update is committed in `88d34308`. Both commits are unsigned under the agreed fallback. The [source comparison](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/final-public-suite-readiness-v1/source-equivalence-88d34308.json) records the Slider baseline: all 141 production files at `88d34308` match CLI `23b6fa98`, and all 63 native pins match native `255e7eaf`. The default-preset correction is proved separately on CLI `826e525e`, with native `255e7eaf` unchanged. Protected working changes are excluded. The later [endpoint classification source batch is committed in `a359af8`](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/slider-endpoint-classification-source-integration-v1/applied.json); it is separate from the native255 runs. The public plan remains **693 cases, 90 suite runs and 66 Swift sources**: iPhone 165, iPad 23 and Duo 505. The normal runner compiled the current app and all 66 sources, including the new raw-touch duration assertion. The business checklist has **55 of 62 items checked**. Current coverage is **337 unique completed cases: 322 passed, 15 failed and 356 without a completed result**. These are the latest accepted outcomes for each case across saved runs, not one full matrix. Native255 supplies the earlier normal results; the six native388 batch-source passes count only for normal batch-text behavior. Native709 supplies the latest Slider, ordinary-iPhone batch/shortcut and Duo keyboard, pair/factory and media outcomes. Automation fixes are committed in `8d295eed` (unsigned). All 13 affected checks passed; original failures remain saved. V8 local ZIP/tar and installation checks passed for CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`, including one installed-wrapper Button check. CLI `826e525e` local delivery remains pending.

The [earlier normal tracked runner built the app and all 66 test sources](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/tracked-core-iphone-v1/scoped-summary.json), then completed nine iPhone core cases: **five passed, four failed**, on CLI `a053711b` / native `255e7eaf`. The two find/type failures were later accessibility verification reads, after the tap commands returned; they do not prove input delivery failed. Drag and Slider reported guest HID barrier timeouts. No shared cause is proved.

The [latest normal 693-case attempt](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/final-public-693-v1/result.json) is INTERRUPTED: **68 completed, 64 passed and four failed**. The ordinary-iPhone core group passed 8/9; its 40% Slider request refused input during a bounded read, after the 75% action. Three handover cases, the first-request held-finger case and all eight ordinary regression cases passed. The shortcut group passed 13/16: one AX read disconnected before its target action; plain `key 7` timed out after Shift+C; then the next compatibility case failed in seed `type AbACd` after that uncertain key. Its selected shortcut was never sent. All owned groups stopped, and source/runtime files stayed unchanged.

The [separate no-build Duo/iPad run](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/duo-hinge-ipad-reuse-v1/run/result.json) completed **42 cases: 34 passed and eight failed**. Duo passed 11/19, including all seven hinge-layout/window cases. Its eight failures were DTX channel disconnects before the target input, with zero target events; they do not prove gesture delivery failed. iPad passed all 23 cases. The run reused the same compiled app, tests and CLI/native; all owned groups stopped and children were reaped, with no source/runtime drift, skips or cancellations. The iPad was restored to its initial shut-down state after the report.

[Later normal iPhone checks](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v1/runs) passed Window1, Keyboard1 and DoubleTap7. [The next normal checks](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v2/runs) passed iPhone1/Duo outer1/inner-tabletop2 switch cases, ordinary Drag7, Pinch8 and NativeState4. Independent-finger bounds first hit the segment guard; the horizontal preset first used pixel dimensions as points. Both test failures remain saved. [All thirteen corrected-case reruns passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/test-corrections-13-proof-v1/runs): six Duo hold/drag/moving-pinch fold/rotation cases, six ordinary independent-finger cases and one Duo horizontal preset. Those replace eight prior failed outcomes without adding unique cases. There were no issues, skips or unsafe input; all owned groups stopped and children were reaped. The broad v1 semantic filter report remains saved and is not counted twice.

Public test sources are integrated and the 66-source build passed; full acceptance remains pending. [Integration and exact proofs](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/raw-input-integration-v1/integration-receipt.json).

Current ordinary-iPhone shortcut flows passed without a library change; their earlier raw key timeouts remain saved. The [unchanged no-flag core Slider case passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/core-slider-current-ui-v2/runs/iphone-core-slider/result.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`: initial 0.2500, then 75 and 40 within the original 0.07 percentage-point limit in app and AX readback. Minimum, already-minimum and six refusal/compatibility cases passed. Maximum reached 100, but its final five-second confirmation could not fit the unchanged budget; three physical percentage cases also failed their time limits. A17 remains open for maximum/performance, and the ordinary-iPhone percentage limits remain under D35. The classification source batch is committed in `a359af8`, with 35 host checks passed. It preserves completed readback when a later read fails; it does not fix latency. The live run did not exercise a late final-read deadline. Original failures remain saved. The native388 recovery branch and raw acknowledgement proposal remain unaccepted; no input replay or timeout increase is accepted.

[Seven earlier passes and one failed case](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/selected-client-exit-native-v4-result.json), [named pinch pass](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/selected-client-exit-native-v5-result.json), [integration receipt](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/client-exit-integration-plan-v1/integration-result.json). A complete named-suite and full-matrix pass remain pending.

Earlier focused evidence is still valid within its recorded candidate: [four hinge-layout cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/hinge-layout-native-v2-result.json) at all four inner rotations; [eight ordinary-iPhone regression cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/ordinary-regression-native-v2-result.json); and [115 host cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/host-v5-all-result.json) on CLI v5. None is a full-suite pass for the later candidates. The saved keyboard timeout and accessibility disconnect still have no confirmed common cause.

Use [the repeatable test guide](playground-testing.md) and [exact coverage map](audits/2026-10-24-manual-automation-map.md). The original 175 mapped rows and archived evidence remain unchanged. [Vendor review](audits/2026-10-24-vendor-failure-review.md) separates source and binary findings from simulator proof. Final local package/install checks passed for CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`; full-suite acceptance remains pending. No upload, push, publication or deployment is authorised. Heavy jobs stay serial, with CPU and memory checks.

The core source audit includes 217 expanded Duo cases, with strict contact, path and recognizer checks, the partly folded swipe and three host-wait cases. The selected Home batch and delayed keyboard cases are also included.

### Duo simulator support

- [x] **D01: Remove the added Duo AX setup requirement.** Complete within the checked normal warm-simulator paths on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`: the current Duo checks found and used controls without an added AX setup command. Channel disconnect failures remain open under D35; no new setup or recovery route is accepted.
- [x] **D02: Fix default scroll presets after rotation.** Complete within the checked default-path scope on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`: omitted-dimension no-flag scrolling after outer landscape rotation revealed and activated a new row. The existing selected inner-landscape horizontal case passed both directions, and ordinary-iPhone explicit-dimension scrolling passed. Defaults now use measured active-display dimensions; explicit dimensions are preserved. Earlier geometry and setup failures remain saved; wider preset positions stay under D35.
- [x] **D03: Set closed, tabletop or open by name.** The current named-position case passed all three settings, with a fresh lookup and a physical Button action after each, on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`.
- [x] **D04: Set a numeric hinge angle.** The current numeric case passed all 18 requested targets, with measured readback and a fresh physical tap after each. This includes actual inner/outer screen changes; wider full-suite acceptance stays under D35.
- [x] **D05: Fix taps after rotation.** Fresh lookups and physical Button actions passed at all four inner rotations and all three supported outer rotations on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`. Old-reference and ongoing-input checks remain separate under D35.
- [x] **D06: Fix swipes after rotation.** Complete within the checked inner-screen scope: after partial folding at rotations 0/90/180/270, a no-flag coordinate swipe scrolled the real document. Other swipe variants and outer-screen coverage remain under D35.
- [x] **D07: Fix drags after rotation.** The current no-flag core cases passed on [inner landscape](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/core-business-closure-plan-v1/runs/duo-core-inner-landscape-drag/result.json) and [outer portrait](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/core-business-closure-plan-v1/runs/duo-core-outer-portrait-drag/result.json), CLI `826e525e` / native `709228dc`. Each app observed the requested start, intermediate movement and release at the endpoint. D07 closes in these two screen scopes. The broader fourteen Duo drag variants stay under D35.
- [x] **D08: Stop old touches when the phone folds or turns.** All six current normal selected-input cases passed: hold, moving drag and moving pinch during fold or rotation. The old path stopped, its original contacts released, no later pad input occurred, and a fresh Button tap changed the app once. Wider agent and retained-input variants remain under D35.
- [x] **D09: Choose the active inner or outer display for supported input.** The current numeric-hinge case proved actual screen switches: old-screen input was refused with zero events, then a fresh active-screen physical tap changed the app. The separate named inactive-screen case failed its AX read before input and remains saved; other input variants stay under D35.
- [x] **D10: Send guarded Siri input.** Selected default and timed presses passed with clean delivery and release on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`; a wrong boot refused input. These are three of the nine passed Hardware cases. Wider positions and interruption checks remain under D35.
- [x] **D11: Send guarded Lock input.** The current selected Lock case passed on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`: two drained events released, backlight changed to inactiveOn and the real clock appeared. Return to the same app and a fresh tap passed. Earlier read and clock-lookup failures remain saved; wider positions stay under D35.
- [x] **D12: Send guarded Apple Pay hardware input.** Both current cases passed on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`: selected command and one batch step each completed four drained hardware events, released input, returned to the same app and passed a fresh tap. Payment-screen testing is excluded; this does not claim Apple Pay simulator UI support.
- [ ] **D13: Enter text and keyboard shortcuts in a selected TextField.** The current keyboard-source group passed 15/16 on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`. All twelve raw/selected literal, file and stdin cases, both focus-loss cases and outer selected editing passed. Inner selected editing failed its AX read before key input, with zero events and no cleanup needed. That failure remains open; this does not show a text-delivery failure.
- [ ] **D14: Stop keyboard input when Duo folds or turns.** The [current no-flag group passed two of four cases](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v6/runs/duo-PlaygroundDuoKeyboardInterruptionTests/result.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`: folding and rotation released the held letter and fresh typing worked. The delayed-sequence fold case failed its external hinge change with uncertain delivery; the next rotation case failed the initial hinge read before input. The [fold-sequence repeat on native `709228dc`](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/raw-key-sequence-layout-recheck-v1/runs/duo-key-sequence-fold/result.json) failed the same external hinge sample read in 29.18 seconds. The [rotation-sequence repeat](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/raw-key-sequence-layout-recheck-v1/runs/duo-key-sequence-rotation/result.json) on the same native failed external rotation confirmation in 29.08 seconds, with `Context hinge sample timed out` and uncertain delivery. It reached the external change; its original attempt stopped at the initial read. These repeats add no case identity and do not show an input-guard defect. [Selected held-Space opening and rotation passed 2/2](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v7/runs/duo-PlaygroundDuoSelectedKeyInterruptionTests/result.json) on native709: input stopped and a fresh Button action worked. The [two current held Shift+A checks](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/held-modifier-navigation-test-correction-v1/effect-review.json) loaded native709, entered uppercase `A` and released both keys with completed barriers. Their later fresh Button taps hit the summary above the scroll viewport and were refused with zero input; lowercase proof did not run. The [later Dismiss correction run](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v9/runs/duo-PlaygroundDuoHeldModifierTests/result.json) also failed both cases: the no-flag Dismiss command returned success, but its focus-loss wait timed out. A [later same-app comparison](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/modifier-dismiss-effect-probe-v1/runs/duo-heldModifierDuringFold/root-inspection-v1/comparison.json) proved one no-flag ID tap at (100,203) returned success with no app contact or focus change; a selected physical tap at that same point delivered DOWN/UP and dismissed the keyboard. The no-flag default has no hold, while the selected physical tap holds for 0.1 seconds. The cause and source fix remain unproved; no display offset or general raw-input failure is inferred. Modifier remains a shadow change and will not enter main. Earlier wrong-library failures remain saved. The [long-type case](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v7/runs/duo-PlaygroundDuoTypingInterruptionTests/result.json) also failed external hinge confirmation. Delayed sequences, long typing and held public-library Shift+A remain open; these failures do not prove an input-guard defect. The CLI shortcut is instant and has no hold option.
- [x] **D15: Keep ordinary standalone typing reliable on Duo.** All six current no-flag literal, file and stdin cases passed on outer portrait and inner landscape, on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`. Each entered exact `aQ7`, recorded three text changes, left zero contacts and kept raw output empty; a fresh physical Button tap then passed. The six selected-source cases also passed. This closes that ordinary typing scope. The unrun Core app cases and other positions stay under D35; the separate inner selected-edit read failure stays under D13.
- [x] **D16: Show the lock screen.** The same current Lock case found the real clock after the backlight change, then returned to the same app and used its Button. This proves the visible simulator effect in the checked position; it does not prove secure lock or a powered-off guest.

Earlier clock-lookup failures remain saved; wider positions stay under D35.

### Improvements from the SimUse comparison

The [current normal iPhone run](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/final-public-693-v1/result.json) passed all **31 agent feature cases** on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`: agent interface four, selectors nine, keyboard observation six and single keys twelve. These cases are already part of the initial 110 completed total above. The [current iPad run](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/duo-hinge-ipad-reuse-v1/run/result.json) also passed the same four basic agent cases. The feature rows below state their checked scope. The [Duo continuation](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v3/runs) passed basic-agent4 and eight of selector9; the remaining saved-reference selector case failed its accessibility read before target input. The eighteen Duo observation/single-key counterparts remain unrun under D35; those groups select outer portrait. These results do not complete all Duo agent coverage. Shortcut failures, uncertain keyboard release and deferred empty-screen recovery remain open.

- [x] **A01: Find a control from part of its label.** Complete in the checked iPhone scope: a partial label produced one real Button callback; a missing label refused input and left app input unchanged. The current Duo positive and absent-label cases also passed; wider checks stay under D35.
- [x] **A02: Find a control with a text pattern.** Complete in the checked iPhone scope: a grouped pattern produced one real Button callback; a pattern matching two Buttons refused input and left app input unchanged. The current Duo positive and duplicate-pattern cases also passed; wider checks stay under D35.
- [x] **A03: Find a control within a chosen screen area.** Complete in the checked iPhone scope: decimal bounds selected the intended Button; excluded bounds refused input and left app input unchanged. The current Duo included/excluded-area cases also passed; wider checks stay under D35.
- [x] **A04: Report keyboard visibility evidence.** All six current iPhone checks passed. Real focus exposed keyboard key evidence and `likelyVisible`; normal dismissal returned `unknown`. Paging and cache reuse kept the same evidence. Duo observation cases remain under D35.
- [x] **A05: Provide the optional RS1 agent interface.** All four current iPhone and four iPad cases passed: compact rows and app/device metadata were valid, a saved reference changed the real Button state, and no-flag accessibility output stayed raw. All four current Duo cases also passed on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`; wider agent coverage stays under D35.
- [x] **A06: Reuse screen snapshots safely.** Current iPhone checks passed reuse, saved-reference input, paging and keyboard metadata; relaunch and layout changes refused old references, then fresh references changed the app. Four further native-state checks passed: duplicate or unnamed rows could not be targeted; a trailing space invalidated an old reference; only the child scroll target moved its list; a bounded read failure sent no input and allowed fresh input. The four basic iPad agent cases also passed. The current Duo basic four also passed. Its separate selector-reference compatibility case failed a fresh AX read before its target action; this remains open under D35.
- [x] **A07: Tell agents which app owns a system alert.** Complete within the checked Photos prompt scope: ordinary-iPhone 3/3 passed on CLI `5a6ae9ce` and Duo outer0 3/3 passed on `c829788d`, both with native `255e7eaf`. The measured owner PID, wrong-PID refusal, current Deny reference/selected ID and closed-prompt app recovery passed. Relevant Photos source and native remain unchanged. Other alert types and full-suite acceptance stay under D35.
- [x] **A08: Install the required helper with Homebrew.** Complete for CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`. The V8 unlinked installation included the helper and resources; both helper architecture/platform checks, strict installed signatures and the installed-wrapper Button check passed. Owned cleanup completed and prior global paths stayed unchanged.
- [x] **A09: Activate a switch through its accessibility action.** The current ordinary-iPhone case, Duo outer case and two Duo inner90/tabletop cases passed on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`. Each accepted one native activation and produced one real switch callback, with released input and other controls unchanged.
- [x] **A10: Check switch state before reporting success.** The same four current cases proved native state false→true, one accepted send/native callback and one actual app state-change callback. Receipts reported the observed value change; no HID event count was used as a substitute for app state. Wider full-suite acceptance stays under D35.
- [x] **A11: Drag from an agent element reference.** All seven current ordinary-iPhone cases passed on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`: the chosen nested list moved, its parent and other controls stayed unchanged, and the contact reached the checked endpoints and released. Stale/PID/bounds refusals and raw/reference-swipe compatibility passed. The planned Duo cases stay under D35.
- [x] **A12: Press and hold an element by its agent reference.** All [seventeen current ordinary-iPhone cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v4/runs/iphone-PlaygroundAgentTouchTests/result.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`. The 0.7-second field hold lasted 0.70088 seconds, released its contact, kept the text and focus, and exposed Select/Select All. Invalid references, timing and partial-input options refused input without changing app state. Raw/selected compatibility and a fresh reference also passed. Planned Duo cases remain under D35.
- [x] **A13: Press a key in an agent-selected text field.** All twelve current iPhone single-key cases passed. Backspace changed the actual field with the exact text callback count, two drained key events and no replay; invalid references, focus and options left app input unchanged. Raw and selected single-key compatibility also passed. Duo single-key cases remain under D35; shortcut and held-input results are separate.
- [x] **A14: Press a keyboard shortcut in an agent-selected field.** Complete within the current ordinary-iPhone scope on CLI `826e525e` / native `709228dc`: agent Shift+A, later lowercase `b`, a fresh field reference, raw Shift+C then `d`, and selected Shift+E produced exact text and app callbacks. Nine refusal cases kept input state unchanged. The [sixteen-case group passed fifteen](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/ordinary-shortcut-triage-v1/final-effect-review.json); one Dismiss AX read failed before input and its intended old-reference refusal did not run. That check, prior raw timeouts and Duo coverage stay under D35. No library fix is inferred.
- [x] **A15: Press several keys in an agent-selected field.** All [seven current ordinary-iPhone cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/normal-suite-continuation-v4/runs/iphone-PlaygroundAgentKeySequenceTests/result.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `255e7eaf`. Two Backspaces changed `ABC` to `A` with two app callbacks and completed key release. Stale references, a wrong app PID and an unfocused field refused input without changing app state. Raw and selected compatibility passed with matching native field values. Planned Duo cases remain under D35; shortcuts and held keys are separate.
- [x] **A16: Double tap a control by its agent reference.** All seven current ordinary-iPhone cases passed on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`: the real recognizer/counter changed once, four input events and two released contacts matched the target; stale/PID/coordinate/batch cases refused invalid input. The planned Duo cases stay under D35.
- [ ] **A17: Move a slider to an end by its agent reference.** The [current twelve-case iPhone group passed eight and failed four](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/slider-agent12-native709-effect-review-v1/review.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `709228dc`. Minimum reached zero with five callbacks; already-minimum sent no action, and six refusal/compatibility cases passed. Maximum reached 100 with ten increments, but its final five-second confirmation could not fit the unchanged 15-second budget. A17 remains open for maximum/performance. The three physical percentage failures stay under D35. The classification change is committed in `a359af8`; 35 host checks passed. No timeout increase or replay is accepted. The live run did not exercise a late final-read deadline; that is a proof limit, not a further requirement. Original native255 failures and earlier endpoint passes remain saved.
- [x] **A18: Pinch an area by its agent reference.** All eight current ordinary-iPhone cases passed on CLI `23b6fa98` / native `255e7eaf`: two contacts moved to the checked endpoints, the real pinch callback/scale changed, both contacts released and other controls stayed unchanged. Invalid area, reference, PID, coordinate and batch cases refused input. The planned Duo cases stay under D35.
- [x] **A19: Run several agent actions in one request.** All [six current ordinary-iPhone cases passed](../build_products/verification/duo-complete-20261024/agent-batch-native709-effect-review-v1/review.json) on CLI `826e525e` / native `709228dc`. The normal batch entered `AB` with two text callbacks and one Button callback; raw and selected flows entered `ABc` and `ABcd`. Contacts released and fresh native field values matched. ## Product checklist

Current status: **56 complete, 2 active open, 2 deferred**. Two items remain active: full-suite acceptance and missing release tracking for no-flag Duo keys/shortcuts. Matrix v5 executed 47 cases: 35 passed and 12 failed, with no skips. All 13 ordinary-iPhone cases passed; Duo core passed 17/18 and optional agent passed 4/4. Outer Backspace timed out, and later layout/interruption cases lost the AX connection. Their cause remains unresolved. Earlier focused passes do not close this result. The unchanged universal Release is `9c383e1b` (CLI `5bd0e336` / native `c3f11c9d`), tested with checkpoint `0e62b532`. Current tests at `b45a60e2` add three separately passed legacy coordinate taps, bringing the default suite to 50; a full 50-case pass is not claimed. The current package passed local unlinked installation, seven strict signatures and a real installed-wrapper app tap, followed by complete removal and restoration. Cold-start stress and conditional empty-control recovery remain deferred. Intel hardware and Apple Pay payment screens are excluded; current-artifact notarization is unverified. No push, publication or deployment occurred. [Latest acceptance record](audits/2026-10-24-playground-acceptance.md), [current handoff](audits/2026-10-22-duo-current-work.md).

The Intel build regression was corrected in `5956171b`: the build accepts arm64, x86_64 or both; the SDK-backed AX class stubs declare both host architectures; and the simulator helper builds and verifies each selected architecture. Earlier universal builds and Rosetta host startup passed. The clean 25e payload also passed its own arm64 and Rosetta host checks, local installation and restoration. Actual Intel hardware/native simulator input remains untested and is excluded by Cam; it is not an active completion item.

Each checkbox is one product outcome. Done applies only to the stated scope. Duo device support and the optional agent improvements are separate lists below. The new 50-case suite covers the listed controls and device changes; it does not cover every command option or custom hinge-driven app layout.

## Technical evidence

### 24 October 2026: full Playground run failed; final installation passed

Test checkpoint `0e62b532` adds the 47-case local acceptance path. Matrix v5 completed with 35 passes and 12 failures, no skips or missing cases. All ordinary-iPhone checks and all optional-agent checks passed. Duo core passed 17/18, including both Slider cases; outer Backspace timed out. Five layout cases and all six interruption cases lost the AX connection. The failed inner-rotation case had real taps at 180 and 270 degrees before its next AX read failed; it remains failed. The other four failed layout cases stopped before their next action, and all six interruption gestures failed before input. The cause is unresolved. A separate held-key release gap has an isolated proposal only: unchanged typing failed before its new route ran, so it was not rolled into main. The exact current package passed local installation and actual installed-wrapper input, then complete removal/restoration. Three additional legacy coordinate-tap checks passed separately and are committed in `b45a60e2`; the current suite has 50 cases, without a full 50-case pass. Earlier focused passes and failed full runs retain their own scope. [Exact command, failures and remaining limits](audits/2026-10-24-playground-acceptance.md).

### 23 October 2026: current scope and automated-test gap

Cam excluded Apple Pay payment UI from the Duo requirements. Test release packaging and delivery without publication or deployment. Current checks are local build/package/install checks. Do not push, tag, publish, deploy, or trigger release jobs with publication side effects. Existing Apple Pay input code, delivery proof and historical failures remain. Earlier dated payment-screen requirements describe the previous scope.

The read-only test audit found a missing repeatable iPhone/Duo Playground suite: [CI](../.github/workflows/test.yml) calls unit-only mode; the [local runner](../test-runner.sh) and [device selector](../scripts/e2e-environment.sh) use one simulator, with no repeated Duo/agent transition matrix. The Playground `.xcodeproj` is [ignored](../.gitignore). Reuse the existing fixtures and make their setup repeatable. This one open item replaces the payment-UI item: **51 Done / 3 Open**, with no new test pass or execution.

### 23 October 2026: agents report the owner of a system alert

Before this fix, the agent error reported processChanged without the actual owner. Debug CLI `41a528aa` / patch `6572c4e1` now preserves `data.observation`: expected PID 36277 and observed PID 37738 in both the refused app-PID read and the completed Open action's failed refresh. The completed two-event tap receipt stayed intact, the capture was invalidated and input was not replayed. A fresh explicit service-PID capture allowed Cancel, then a fresh app capture succeeded. [Acceptance](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/agent-modal-owner-20261023/diagnostic-source-v1/scoped-diagnostic-acceptance-v1.json).

Root ran the [bound entry](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/agent-modal-owner-20261023/diagnostic-source-v1/manual-proof-v3/run-v3.json) once: capture app → Open → refuse the app PID at the alert → confirm the visible owner → capture that explicit service PID → Cancel → capture the returned app. Both intentional taps completed; the 46-command run passed in 150.80 seconds at 16.896 GiB, with no stop/crash/signals, reaped processes, broker exit and F Shutdown. Build/stage passed on unchanged Debug native `f08c862b`; this is separate from the notarized Release archive built from `a33a97e8`. Wallet controls need a suitable runtime; the same-PID empty-tree enhancement is deferred without a current failure case. Intel builds are incomplete: the build rejects non-arm64 and the new AX stub exports are arm64-only. Compatible Intel SDK/runtime export evidence and reviewed build changes are required before verification. These limits are separate from accepted Duo arm64 work. Batch/quarantine propagation has source review only. **51 Done / 3 Open**. [Saved result](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/agent-modal-owner-20261023/diagnostic-source-v1/manual-proof-v3/proof-v3/verification.json).

The exact Developer ID signed, Apple-accepted arm64 archive `d9d91614` / extracted CLI `3e751271` passed one selected outer-portrait slider action: 50→63.27345276 for 63.25, 37 app callbacks and a released contact. This is the single progress record for Duo implementation and integration. The broad audit is stopped. On 21 October 2026, Cam authorized parallel worktrees, bounded simulator copies and local integration commits into main. No push is authorized.

States: TODO = work remains; IMPLEMENTED = code is integrated but verification is incomplete; VERIFIED = the stated scope passed on the integrated candidate; BLOCKED = the exact named dependency prevents progress. N/A = the platform design excludes the case; it is not an unfinished AXe feature or a failed test. RECORDED = a historical observation with no reproducible current defect; it does not block core feature acceptance. ISOLATED = a native experiment passed outside the library; product integration is still pending. A group is VERIFIED only when its complete applicable scope is verified. Prior isolated results are inputs, not proof of the integrated build.

Earlier isolated touch proof, 22 October: source 09990e30, runtime-v1 CLI SHA256 ccb813f1decb62f97254a7227d4dc9706ee5291ca105f642b05b72145f676be7, unchanged rebuilt fixture 81c14060. The [ordinary-iPhone two-case proof](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/touch-owned-parts-20261022/ordinary-iphone/proof-v2/verification.json) and [fresh-Duo inner270 two-case proof](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/touch-owned-parts-20261022/fresh-duo-post-restart/proof-v1/verification.json) passed one tap callback and one completed pan each, including exact same-contact BEGIN/MOVE/END and no inserted MOVE at R. Both owned brokers exited and guests shut down. The [ordinary guard](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/touch-owned-parts-20261022/ordinary-iphone/guard-v2/result.json) and [post-restart Duo guard](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/touch-owned-parts-20261022/fresh-duo-post-restart/guard-v1/result.json) returned exit 0, no stop reason and reaped children. The Duo run used the authorised raised resource policy. Total Duo support and the remaining touch cases are not complete; earlier failures and setup refusals remain unchanged.

The earlier isolated batch candidate built CLI 65d830dea6df6a7c85fd2b249b0ab8cc37f47f6642712ff994130374a87e6531 and passed 173 existing checks in 19 suites. [Build/source receipt](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/batch-owned-20261022/build-and-checks.json), [test guard](/Volumes/Developer/AXe/build_products/verification/batch-owned-20261022/existing-tests-guard-v1/result.json). Native batch effects, stopping and ownership acceptance were pending at that checkpoint. The current main acceptance above records the later batch proof.

The source-only sim-use comparison supplied improvement ideas, not a speed or reliability ranking. Compact agent state, references, cache/invalidation and helper installation are integrated. Independent two-finger straight paths, explicit clipboard staging and one controlled Unicode Paste workflow are integrated within their recorded scope. Wider app/menu support, command support, empty/hosted AX recovery and inset-scene work remain. The local viewer, extra video formats and platform expansion remain deferred.

The earlier technical register has 17 parent groups and 77 scoped subitems after adding D08.LEGACY.PATHS: 68 VERIFIED, three IMPLEMENTED, three TODO, one historical ISOLATED, one N/A and one RECORDED. These overlap and are not a completion percentage. D07 is VERIFIED; the other 16 groups retain the gaps below. Historical results remain tied to their exact candidates.

Earlier swipe/drag combined-main verification: arm64 build, 164 existing checks in 18 suites, and six native cases passed on CLI SHA256 330916a44750ed70fef5aa7a8cbef617c5e16f6442a183b8e90f8f9089399328. Both new interruption checks released the contact, then tapped the current button within 0.5 point. Those combined-main runs used limits of app 8 GiB, simulators 10 GiB and owned commands 4 GiB, with the existing CPU limits. No push.

## Core feature status from accepted native records

Native passes below belong to the exact recorded candidates. The switch candidate verifies the five named physical switch cases and one normal Button. The rotation candidate, CLI5441d467/wire21, adds public left/right rotation with seven successful fresh Button taps and three rejection checks (D04.ROTATION). The latest CLI5f4e7195/FBS8a614ab3/wire21 fixes holds and retained contacts across changes; eight native interruption/refind cases passed (D03.HELDINPUT). On 21 October, the unchanged candidate also passed outward-pinch closure and inward-pinch rotation, including both-contact release and two fresh named-button effects (D03.MOVINGPINCH). On 20 October, the same unchanged CLI also passed the one inner90/hinge90 swipe in D08.SWIPE.INNER90. This does not rerun all earlier features or diagnose the single historical final-read timeout. That observation is recorded and does not block core work. Broader rows stay open only for the stated remaining scope.

On 21 October, the parallel round also passed two moving-twist interruptions, eleven outer270 input cases, selected side-button completion, eighteen hinge observations with nine old-screen rejection/refind checks, two held-Space cleanup/refind sequences and twenty ordinary iPhone/iPad actions. All worker commits are reviewed and merged into local main. The five saved-data validators passed from main. No new product fix was required for those cases. Reports and remaining scope: [parallel handoff](audits/2026-10-21-duo-parallel-work.md).

| Core area | Proven and checked off | Remaining work |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Find and tap after rotation/folding | D07.IDTAP: all four inner orientations and outer portrait/both landscapes. D03.OPEN.REFIND: find and tap the field after opening. D03.FOLDLAYOUT/FOLDTABLETOP: find and use moved controls in real side-by-side and top/bottom layouts. | Wider roles/selectors and transition combinations. Outer upside-down is N/A under the platform policy below. |
| Complete gestures | Recorded tap, hold, double tap, drag and complete multi-touch checks cover all four inner orientations and outer portrait/both landscapes. Exact candidates and cases are recorded below. Retained library pair idle release passed D09.RETAINED. | Other transition combinations, failure paths and wider option coverage; no-flag swipe/drag acceptance is recorded in D08.LEGACY.PATHS. |
| Typing and controls | Bounded literal/file/stdin, keys/combinations/sequences on both panels; slider endpoints; explicit coordinate switch input; named physical switch input after opening, rotation, hinge90 and closing (D10.SWITCH). | Wider roles/options; semantic switch activation failed and needs a fix (D10.SWITCH.SEMANTIC). |
| Duo controls | Explicit panel selection, inactive-panel rejection, hinge read/write and refind after opening. Public rotate left/right now passed all seven applicable positions with fresh named-button taps. Rotation rejected an old boot, an inactive screen and a real held touch. Nine hinge-driven screen switches rejected the old target and passed fresh taps (D04.BOUNDARY). | The wider transition/input matrix and post-dispatch cancellation remain. Bounded held-input changes passed D03.HELDINPUT. |

Outer upside-down portrait is **N/A**, not BLOCKED: Apple documents that iPhones without a Home button do not support that interface orientation, and says Duo outer-display orientation follows ordinary iPhone behavior. The measured Device Hub cycle matches that policy. Inner upside-down input has already passed. [Apple orientation policy](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/uikit/uiviewcontroller/supportedinterfaceorientations), [Apple Duo display guidance](https://developer.apple.com/videos/play/tech-talks/111461/).

## Existing-feature inventory

Inventory completed once from the initial registration: 20 commands, including the hidden broker. Integration adds public `hinge` and `rotate` (22 total); selected broker wire9 was followed by wire10 for keyboard input and wire11 for the distinct point-read payload, beside legacy wire2. Wire12 adds the explicit edge start inset. Wire13 adds standalone selected Home and its typed execution/cleanup result; legacy wire2 is unchanged. Later wire14 added the type filter and wire15 added value selection. Wire16 carries the typed simulator tap style and rejects old wire15. Wire17 added automatic style and rejected old wire16. Wire18 added the exact selected slider type filter. Wire19 added exact-label slider endpoints and batch stdin. Wire20 added selected standalone/batch side-button; wire21 added rotation control, wire22 added selected tap waiting and wire23 added optional agent mode. Main 55939716 uses wire24 for measured no-flag tap admission. The isolated 02136849 swipe/drag checkpoint uses wire25 and is not merged. Legacy wire2 remains unchanged.

| Existing commands or library features | Checklist IDs | Existing boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| list-simulators | D01, D02, D16 | Device catalogue and simulator set. |
| describe-ui; library AX tree, exact/label/value/point lookup, typed values/actions | D02, D03, D05, D10 | Selected acquisition and bounded explicit actions are integrated; remaining roles/options are listed in D05/D10. |
| tap; touch | D02, D03, D06, D07, D15 | Coordinate/selector taps, tap style, wait/poll, complete hold. |
| swipe; drag; gesture | D02, D03, D06, D08, D15 | Explicit paths, duration/steps, four scroll and four edge presets. |
| Library two-contact/pinch factories; isolated pinch/rotate/double/two-finger routes | D06, D07, D09, D15 | Complete-event routes and bounded retained pair idle release are integrated; wider transitions, options and failure injection remain open (D09.RETAINED). |
| slider | D02, D03, D06, D10 | Selector, coordinate drag, observed-value verification. |
| type; key; key-sequence; key-combo | D02, D03, D10, D11, D15 | Literal/file/stdin, keys and modifiers. Selected focus guards are integrated, with bounded native proof in D11. |
| button; library orientation, shake, focus, URL and lifecycle | D01, D04, D12, D15 | Home, lock, side button, Siri and Apple Pay. Hinge and power need separate proof. |
| screenshot; stream-video; record-video | D02, D03, D06, D13 | PNG; raw/MJPEG/ffmpeg/BGRA; MP4. JPEG contract fixed below. |
| batch | D02, D14, D15 | Initial legacy steps: tap, swipe, gesture, touch, type, button, key, key-sequence, key-combo, sleep; legacy drag/slider/media remain unsupported. Integrated selected routes also include drag, slider endpoints, stdin, Home and side-button, plus a separate host sleep without input ownership. Bounded selected side-button native completion is recorded in D12.SIDE.SELECTED. Other hardware buttons, retained tokens and media remain open. |
| agent; init; optional --agent on describe-ui/tap/type/swipe | D14, D17 | The four-command provider-free stateful layer is integrated. It is separate from the autonomous axe agent loop and its unrelated local edits. Other --agent commands remain open. |
| hid-broker (hidden) | D02, D03, D15, D17 | Same-user transport, boot-aware reuse, no resend after a lost response. |
| Library app install/launch/kill, settings, permissions, appearance, status bar, location, memory warning, notifications, media import | D01, D12, D16, D17 | Retain the existing library surface. |
| Library file containers, keychain, processes/launchctl, logs/crashes, debugger/DAP, Instruments/XCTrace, REPL and XCTest | D12, D16, D17 | Retain the existing library surface. |
| FBControlCore; physical FBDeviceControl; XCTestBootstrap; companion, shims and private headers | D16, D17 | Preserve ordinary/physical backends and matching framework builds. |
| Package.swift/resolved, VersionPlugin, build script, bundled skill and docs | D17 | Four XCFrameworks use pinned ignored IDB source. Track the library delta and its application. |

## Verification matrix

Every applicable capability needs application effects plus state readback. A screenshot or command exit alone does not prove interaction. Record the exact integrated candidate and command/test result in this file. Cases excluded by documented platform design are N/A, with the reason and source stated. A failure to reach a supported setup remains BLOCKED with its exact cause. Neither state is an input test pass.

| Scenario | Overall state | Proven scope | Remaining scope |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Outer screen, closed | TODO | Exact-ID tap: portrait and both landscapes. Complete gesture/typing cases are recorded in D07–D11. Upside-down portrait is N/A. | Remaining applicable feature/pose cases. |
| Inner screen, fully open | TODO | Exact-ID tap, complete gestures and multi-touch at all four rotations; bounded text/control cases. | Wider feature/role/options coverage. |
| Intermediate hinge | TODO | Recorded hinge90 input, focus, slider and scroll cases. | Other representative angles and app layouts. |
| Continuous hinge endpoints | IMPLEMENTED | Closed0 and full-open180 with measured readiness and native input effects. | Boundary coverage and other endpoint combinations. |
| Active-screen boundaries | TODO | Closing/opening endpoints and inactive-panel rejection. | Both sides of each boundary in both movement directions. |
| Rotate/fold/open transitions | TODO | Exact-ID refind/tap across seven positions; field refind/tap after opening. | Remaining feature/transition combinations. |
| Interrupted/stale input | TODO | Recorded stale-target rejection and bounded release cases in D15. | Remaining cancellation/transition cases. |
| Ordinary iPhone | TODO | Bounded portrait legacy and selected input in D16. | Wider orientations/features and current-candidate regressions. |
| iPad | TODO | Bounded portrait legacy and selected input in D16. | Wider orientations/features and current-candidate regressions. |

## Latest held-input fixes: 20 October 2026

D03.HELDINPUT is checked off for eight final native cases on CLI5f4e7195/FBS8a614ab3/wire21. Each fold/rotation interrupted a drag, complete single hold, complete two-finger hold or retained single touch. Old retained-token requests sent zero events and rejected before input. Opening and closing changed the selected panel correctly.

Two faults were reproduced before changes. AXe waited until the end of a six-second hold before sending its owned release, even though UIKit had already cancelled the app touch. An initial app-only cancellation check is therefore superseded and is not accepted as AXe release-timing proof. The library diagnostic recorded5.67seconds before the fix and0.53/0.79seconds for isolated fold/rotation candidates. The corrected wait checks the existing local feed during delays, without new full context processes. The retained owner also kept its token until another request; its idle maintenance now releases invalid input and clears the token while blocking reentry. Uncertain cleanup keeps the common lock and recovery record.
